L2977: 48	function topologicalSort<N extends Operator<IOperator>>(
L2978: 49	  nodes: NodeJSON<OpType>[],
L2979: 50	  edges: Edge<N, N>[]

L2981: 52	  const sortedNodes: NodeJSON<OpType>[] = []
L2982: 53	  const visitedNodes = new Set<string>()
L2983: 54	
L2984: 55	  function traverse(node: NodeJSON<OpType>) {
L2985: 56	    if (visitedNodes.has(node.id)) {
L2986: 57	      return
L2987: 58	    }
L2988: 59	
L2989: 60	    visitedNodes.add(node.id)
L2990: 61	
L2991: 62	    const outgoingEdges = edges.filter(edge => edge.source === node.id)
L2992: 63	    for (const edge of outgoingEdges) {
L2993: 64	      const targetNode = nodes.find(n => n.id === edge.target)
L2994: 65	      if (targetNode) {
L2995: 66	        traverse(targetNode)
L2996: 67	      }
L2997: 68	    }

L3004: 75	
L3005: 76	  for (const node of sourceNodes) {
L3006: 77	    traverse(node)

L3008: 79
